Keep numeric types numeric.\n        current_f = float(current) if current is not None else 0.0\n        daily_f = float(daily) if daily is not None else 0.0\n\n        rows.append(\n            CurrentStaffRow(\n                care_unit=str(unit).strip(),\n                current_staff_hours=current_f,\n                daily_required_hours=daily_f,\n            )\n        )\n\n    return as_of, end, rows\n\n\ndef _read_incoming_shifts(wb) -> List[IncomingShiftRow]:\n    ws = wb[\"Incoming Shifts\"]\n    header_row, headers = _find_header_row(ws, [\"Care_Unit\", \"Shift Date\", \"Staff Hours\"])\n\n    col_unit = headers[\"Care_Unit\"]\n    col_date = headers[\"Shift Date\"]\n    col_hours = headers[\"Staff Hours\"]\n\n    shifts: List[IncomingShiftRow] = []\n    for r in range(header_row + 1, ws.max_row + 1):\n        unit = ws.cell(r, col_unit).value\n        d = _to_date(ws.cell(r, col_date).value)\n        hrs = ws.cell(r, col_hours).value\n\n        if unit is None or str(unit).strip() == \"\":\n            continue\n        if d is None:\n            continue\n        if hrs is None or hrs == \"\":\n            continue\n\n        shifts.append(\n            IncomingShiftRow(\n                care_unit=str(unit).strip(),\n                shift_date=d,\n                staff_hours=float(hrs),\n            )\n        )\n\n    return shifts\n\n\ndef _read_ratio(wb) -> float:\n    ws = wb[\"Ratio\"]\n\n    # Look for the label, then take the value directly below it.\n    for r in range(1, ws.max_row + 1):\n        for c in range(1, ws.max_column + 1):\n            v = ws.cell(r, c).value\n            if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip() == \"Hours_Per_Shift_Block\":\n                ratio_val = ws.cell(r + 1, c).value\n                if ratio_val is None or ratio_val == \"\":\n                    raise ValueError(\"Ratio value (hours per shift block) is blank\")\n                return float(ratio_val)\n\n    raise ValueError(\"Could not find 'Hours_Per_Shift_Block' in Ratio sheet\")\n\n\ndef _iso(d: Optional[date]) -> Optional[str]:\n    return d.isoformat() if d is not None else None\n\n\ndef generate() -> None:\n    src_wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(SOURCE_XLSX, data_only=True)\n\n    as_of, planning_end, current_rows = _read_current_staffing(src_wb)\n    incoming_rows = _read_incoming_shifts(src_wb)\n    ratio = _read_ratio(src_wb)\n\n    remaining_days = (planning_end - as_of).days\n\n    #
